### [60] [Autonomous LLM Agents & CTFs: A Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21497)
*Youness Bouchari,Matteo Boffa,Marco Mellia,Idilio Drago,Thanh Minh Bui,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过30个Web CTF挑战（涵盖14种漏洞类型）重新评估了LLM智能体在 offensive security 任务中的表现。主要发现：1) 通用智能体claude-code表现接近专门设计的架构（19/30）；2) 所有智能体在相同类别上表现不佳，尚未达到人类水平；3) 结构化编排专业化角色优于单体设计，能提高一致性并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称LLM智能体在CTF挑战中达到接近人类水平的成功率，本文旨在重新审视这些结论，通过更系统的评估验证其真实性。

Method: 设计了从简单到复杂的多种智能体架构，在30个Web CTF挑战（14种漏洞类型）上测试，使用多个LLM作为骨干，并与通用智能体claude-code进行比较。

Result: claude-code解决19/30任务；所有智能体在相同挑战类别上表现不佳；结构化编排专业化角色优于单体设计，提高了运行一致性和执行效率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在 offensive security 任务中尚未达到人类水平，通用智能体是强有力的基线，结构化架构设计是提升性能的关键方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed to automate offensive security tasks, with recent studies reporting near human-level success rates in Capture-the-Flag (CTF) challenges. We here revisit these results, providing a second look at these claims. We engineer different agent architectures of increasing complexity and modularity on 30 web-based CTFs challenges spanning 14 vulnerability classes. We instantiate these agents with multiple LLM backbones, and compare them with claude-code, a general-purpose agent that automatically determines its internal architecture. Our evaluation yields three main findings. First, claude-code achieves performance comparable to the engineered architectures (19/30 solved tasks), suggesting that general-purpose agents are strong baselines for offensive security tasks. Second, both our architectures and claude-code struggle in the same challenge categories, revealing persistent barriers that keep current agents below human-level capability. Third, by leveraging our manually designed architectures we can systematically measure the impact of additional components, finding that structured orchestration of specialized roles outperforms monolithic designs, improving run-to-run consistency, and reducing execution costs.

</details>


### [61] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于系统性的nonce重用漏洞，导致攻击者可以通过被动观察链上签名，利用线性代数方法完全恢复私钥。该问题源于searcher在密封投标拍卖中为追求亚秒级响应时间而使用可预测的nonce模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECDSA签名的安全性严重依赖正确生成nonce，但在Polygon MEV生态系统中，searcher为满足密封投标拍卖的亚秒级延迟要求，采用了可预测的nonce模式，导致系统性nonce重用漏洞。

Method: 1) 分析链上数据，识别searcher使用的可预测nonce模式；2) 建立紧凑的线性方程组攻击框架，涵盖跨钱包nonce碰撞的极端情况；3) 在Polygon链上提供可利用模式的具体证据。

Result: 发现searcher因协议延迟压力而使用可预测nonce模式，形成签名间的线性关系，使得被动攻击者可利用基础代数恢复私钥。

Conclusion: 协议诱导的延迟压力会导致生产级区块链系统出现灾难性密码学失败，单个实现错误会同时危及多个账户的安全。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [62] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY，一个包含73,610个二进制文件、覆盖248个开源项目的统一框架，集成了跨编译器构建、多版本历史和CVE标签，并通过三项分析验证其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库通常只捕获一到两个二进制变异维度（如仅提供跨编译器构建而无时间维度，或仅提供单构建二进制文件的CVE标签），没有将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合到可查询的结构中。

Method: 构建包含73,610个二进制文件的语料库，覆盖248个开源项目，使用GCC、Clang和MSVC编译器在Linux和Windows上以多种优化级别编译，包含多年历史构建。每个二进制文件在数据库中索引，链接到源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和易受攻击函数。

Result: 通过三项分析验证：1) 三阶段LLM基准测试评估LLM推理漏洞能力；2) 比较MalConv、jTrans和TLSH嵌入的聚类效果；3) 贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性的贡献因素。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY成功将跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签整合为统一的可查询框架，为二进制分析研究提供了结构化数据集。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [63] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个多LLM协作的推理驱动框架，通过将提示发现建模为非凸优化问题，高效生成越狱提示，在更低的计算成本下实现更高攻击成功率，且生成的提示仅有不到1%被标记为有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽已广泛应用，但仍易受到越狱攻击（基于提示的攻击绕过安全过滤器），需要更高效的自动化攻击框架来发现未检测到的漏洞，从而主动加强模型安全性。

Method: 提出THREAT框架，将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，采用推理驱动的迭代搜索循环协调多个LLM协同工作，以降低运行时间并提高攻击有效性。

Result: THREAT在多个数据集和模型架构上实现了更高的攻击成功率且计算成本更低；生成的提示仅有不到1%被标记为有害，而对应未修改的提示拒绝率约为50%。

Conclusion: THREAT揭示了现有对齐LLM中此前未被检测到的漏洞，可作为主动加强基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [64] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FuzzingBrain V2，一个基于多智能体系统的自动化漏洞检测框架，结合OSS-Fuzz模糊测试、控制流抽象（Suspicious Point）和MCP工具，在AIxCC 2025数据集上达到90%检测率，并在实际部署中发现29个零日漏洞（2个已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测存在三大问题：高误报率且无法可重复验证、漏洞定位粒度不优（函数级粒度太粗/行级粒度缺乏上下文）、难以推理复杂跨函数依赖和触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于OSS-Fuzz的全自动分析确保漏洞可模糊测试复现；(2) 提出Suspicious Point控制流抽象实现最优粒度漏洞定位；(3) 逻辑驱动分层函数分析+双层模糊测试提升资源约束下的函数覆盖率；(4) MCP基础静态/动态分析工具+上下文工程增强复杂漏洞推理能力。

Result: AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上检测率90%（40个中检测到36个）；实际部署中在12个开源项目发现29个零日漏洞，全部被维护者确认并修复，2个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过结合模糊测试的可复现性与LLM的推理能力，有效解决了自动化漏洞检测中的可验证性、定位粒度和复杂推理三大挑战，展示了多智能体系统在真实漏洞挖掘中的实用价值。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [65] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Arrow-native DataFrame引擎Polar在Intel SGX2可信执行环境下的TPC-H基准测试性能，发现端到端开销稳定在1.49-1.56×，但表加载和查询计算呈现出截然不同的开销特征，且惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE机密分析研究多聚焦于SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX1代，缺乏对Arrow-native DataFrame引擎在最新SGX2平台上的系统性评估，尤其是数据加载路径与查询计算的开销解耦分析。

Method: 在Gramine+Intel SGX2环境下运行Polars引擎，使用TPC-H SF30数据集（约22-73GB）和Azure Blob Storage，设计了标准TPC-H功率分数和仅查询变体两种评估方式，并对比了惰性与急切API的性能差异。

Result: 端到端开销为1.49-1.56×，但查询仅开销从1.51-1.52×降至1.43-1.44×，表加载开销从2.27×升至4.07×；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×，急切API在41GB以上发生OOM错误。

Conclusion: SGX2可以支持Arrow-native分析处理，安全开销与SQL引擎相当，但数据加载路径的放大效应和API级优化是端到端性能的首要决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [66] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，明确了路径长度和循环结构的显式特征，证明了图具有强规律性，包括给定长度的循环数量恒定以及随 $k_1, k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力学复杂性及在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要，本文旨在将先前在素幂环上的研究推广到复合模数情形。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式模2和模3幂的新性质，将环中每个元素视为顶点，多项式定义映射为有向边，对路径长度和循环结构进行显式刻画。

Result: 函数图展现出强规律性：给定长度的循环数量恒定，且随 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增长分支模式可预测；二元和三元分量的复杂性并未破坏图的结构规则性。

Conclusion: 研究结果扩展了先前在素幂环上的工作，为数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持其密码学应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [67] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则，以绕过沙箱环境中的逃逸检测。通过对334个真实恶意软件样本的评估，ABLE实现了79%的绕过成功率，迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例，相比现有分析平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沙箱逃逸检测依赖手动构建的绕过规则，需要对每个逃逸机制进行深度逆向工程，无法应对快速演变的逃逸技术，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: ABLE系统利用大语言模型分析因潜在逃逸行为而终止的恶意软件执行轨迹，采用多种推理策略生成针对性YARA绕过规则，并引入自动消毒管道和反馈驱动的迭代优化来修正语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本上，ABLE实现了79%的绕过成功率；迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例；相比现有分析平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类，并暴露了先前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: ABLE证明了大语言模型在自动化生成沙箱逃逸绕过规则方面的有效性，显著提升了恶意软件分析的覆盖率和效率，为应对快速演变的逃逸技术提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [68] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz 是一个基于LLM的自动化测试工具生成系统，通过"四项原则"框架（逻辑正确性、API协议合规、安全边界尊重、入口点充分性）在生成阶段系统性地确保harness的正确性，在23个开源项目中发现了42个漏洞（其中29个已修复/确认，含3个CVE），并将误报率控制在4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模糊测试工具自动生成技术无法系统性地确保生成harness的正确性，逻辑错误、API误用及生命周期违规在源代码层面未被检测。随着LLM驱动的大规模harness生成，不受控的质量问题将把规模优势转变为负担。

Method: 提出四项原则（P1-P4）框架作为首个源代码级别的harness正确性定义，包含数学规范与可实现的检查；基于该框架构建自主LLM智能体，通过"生成-检查-修复"循环在模糊测试开始前确保harness满足P1-P4要求。

Result: 在23个开源项目（C/C++、Java、JavaScript）中发现42个漏洞，29个已修复/确认（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（4.8%误报率）；内置检查自动拦截58次因harness导致的崩溃；对70个项目的586个生产级harness审计发现53个违规（45个已确认，35个已修复）。

Conclusion: QuartetFuzz通过系统性的正确性保证机制，有效提升了自动化harness生成的质量，验证了在生成阶段前置检查相比事后修复更能降低误报率，展示了基于原则的LLM智能体在软件安全测试中的应用价值。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [69] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出baseSPIDER和SPIDER两种隐私信息检索(PIR)方案。baseSPIDER在单服务器有状态客户端场景下实现了与最先进方案渐近最优的通信复杂度，并改善了常数因子；SPIDER是首个无需服务器特殊配合的单服务器PIR方案，通过简单转换baseSPIDER实现，消除了部署障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIR方案在通信复杂度上虽已达到渐近最优，但常数因子仍有优化空间；同时大多数方案依赖服务器特殊API或协议特定交互，部署门槛高。本文旨在设计更简洁、常数因子更优且易于部署的PIR方案。

Method: baseSPIDER采用单服务器有状态客户端模型，通过预处理和存储提示(hints)实现查询，在通信复杂度上优化了常数因子。SPIDER通过简单变换将baseSPIDER适配到默认服务器接口，无需服务器任何阶段配合，仅依赖常规索引访问。该变换还可推广到其他三种近期PIR方案。

Result: baseSPIDER匹配了最先进方案的渐近最优通信复杂度并改进了常数因子，尤其适用于大条目数据库。SPIDER是首个在默认服务器接口下实现隐私保护的单服务器PIR方案，消除了专用API、辅助服务器状态等部署障碍。

Conclusion: baseSPIDER和SPIDER在通信效率和部署便捷性上取得了进展。SPIDER的设计消除了传统PIR方案的部署障碍，可即时应用于现有系统；其变换方法具有通用性，可为其他PIR方案提供同类适配。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [70] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个利用大语言模型（LLM）从自然语言安全问题自动生成可执行查询代码的系统，仅需一次LLM调用即可，相比人工脚本编写，在复杂跨事件查询上错误率降低3倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析人员查询系统日志时面临两难：使用结构化日志查询需要为每种格式构建解析器，工程成本高；直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志虽然成本低，但要求分析人员了解每种日志的消息变体，且无法表达安全调查所需的多行时间序列查询。

Method: Sieve通过轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文来引导大语言模型（LLM），每个查询仅需一次LLM调用，随后进行确定性执行，从而生成可执行查询代码。

Result: 在5种日志类型上评估133个安全查询，Sieve在复杂时间序列和跨事件查询上的错误率比人工脚本编写降低3倍以上，在多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码可以弥合结构化日志查询的表达性与直接处理原始文件的即时性之间的差距。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [71] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一个基于生成器的单次保护框架，通过在合成图像中注入频率感知的不可察觉扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时有效防止模型窃取，实现了功效、保真度和效率之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭重生成服务通过API部署时面临模型窃取威胁：攻击者可通过反复查询收集合成图像并训练替代模型，实现未经授权的知识蒸馏和能力复制。现有防御需要在保护图像的同时保持视觉保真度、提供显式扰动幅度控制并高效扩展到大规模输出。

Method: WaveGuard采用单次、基于生成器的保护框架，使用频率感知的扰动生成器向合成图像注入结构化且不可察觉的扰动。该方法在用户指定的扰动预算下运行，保持图像的感知效用，同时降低被保护图像作为未授权学生模型训练数据的效用。

Result: 在WikiArt相关合成输出蒸馏设置下的广泛实验表明，WaveGuard实现了功效-保真度-效率之间的有利平衡，具备显式的不可察觉性控制，并在保护效率上获得显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard能够有效防御针对闭重生成服务的模型窃取攻击，在保持输出图像视觉质量的同时，通过频率感知扰动大幅降低未授权蒸馏的效果，且具备高效的大规模部署能力。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [72] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子力学栅极隧穿泄漏电流的物理不可克隆函数，专为超低功耗医疗物联网（IoMT）设备设计，无需外部激励或稳定电路，在65nm CMOS工艺下实现96.04nW/bit的超低功耗、0.9999998的熵值和0.5001的FHD，且在0.9–1.3V和0–100°C范围内可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备面临物理篡改和伪造威胁，现有PUF（如存储器型、环形振荡器型、仲裁器型）因额外电路开销、功耗高或稳定性差，不适用于超低功耗的可穿戴和植入式医疗设备，亟需一种极低功耗、高可靠性的轻量级硬件安全方案。

Method: 利用标准CMOS工艺中由工艺偏差引起的量子力学栅极隧穿泄漏电流作为熵源，设计带有伪电阻I-V前端转换电路的差分读出结构，将皮安级泄漏电流差异转换为数字响应，无需外部激励或稳定电路，静态偏置即可工作。

Result: 在65nm CMOS工艺仿真中，QT-PUF达到熵值0.9999998、FHD 0.5001、平均功耗96.04nW/bit（19.21fJ/bit），在0.9–1.3V和0–100°C范围内可靠运行，1.0–1.3V及10–70°C下平均BER低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF通过利用本征量子隧穿效应实现了极低功耗和高稳定性，解决了IoMT设备信任根问题，适用于典型植入式设备的工作条件范围。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [73] [Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122)
*Yutong Liu,Chenyi Wang,Ming F. Li,Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustFlip攻击，利用协同感知中一致性检测防御机制的漏洞，通过部署物理对抗物体使良性车辆间产生不一致观测，导致防御系统错误地降低目标车辆的信任评分并将其排除出协作，从而降低整体感知性能。同时提出TrustReflect作为初步缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知防御系统通过交叉车辆一致性检测和信任评估来抵御数据篡改攻击，但这些防御机制本身存在新攻击面——攻击者可利用一致性检测机制反向操纵针对良性车辆的信任评分。

Method: TrustFlip攻击在不注入虚假数据的前提下，部署物理对抗物体使良性车辆之间产生真实但不一致的观测结果，防御系统将这些不一致错误归因于目标车辆，导致其信任评分下降并最终被降权或排除出协作流程。

Result: 在多种协同感知架构与防御机制下，TrustFlip在高达87.7%的场景中成功将目标良性车辆从协作中移除，平均精度（AP）下降最多达13%。TrustReflect作为缓解措施，将攻击成功率降低35-100%。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于一致性检测的协同感知防御机制存在根本性脆弱性，恶意攻击可反向利用该机制削弱良性车辆的贡献。TrustReflect通过自我反思机制标记争议区域为不确定并排除出信任评估，提供了有效初步防护。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) enables connected and autonomous vehicles to share sensor data and jointly reason about their environment. To defend against adversaries that fabricate or manipulate shared data, existing systems employ cross-vehicle inconsistency detection and trust estimation, penalizing vehicles whose observations conflict with the majority. In this work, we show that these defenses themselves introduce a new attack surface. We present TrustFlip, a novel attack that weaponizes consistency-based defenses to poison the trust assigned to benign vehicles. Instead of injecting false data into the collaboration pipeline, it deploys physical adversarial objects that are genuine but induce inconsistent observations among benign vehicles. The resulting inconsistencies are misattributed by the defense to the targeted vehicle, causing its trust score to degrade and eventually leading to its downweighting or exclusion from collaboration. Consequently, the system loses reliable sensing contributors, degrading perception capability and potentially inducing safety-critical failures. We evaluate TrustFlip across multiple collaborative perception architectures and defense mechanisms. Our results show that state-of-the-art defenses can be significantly affected: the attack removes the targeted benign vehicle from collaboration in up to 87.7% of scenarios and drops Average Precision (AP) by up to 13%. As an initial mitigation, we introduce TrustReflect, a lightweight self-reflection mechanism that marks disputed regions as uncertain and excludes them from trust evaluation, reducing the attack success rate by 35-100%.

</details>


### [74] [Benchmarking Autonomous Agents against Temporal, Spatial, and Semantic Evasions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22321)
*Jianan Ma,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yaoxiang Bian,Jialuo Chen,Jingyi Wang,Xiaofang Yang,Shiwen Cui,Changhua Meng,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM智能体系统的多维规避框架，包含时间、空间和语义三种隐蔽攻击向量，并构建了A3S-Bench基准数据集。实验表明该框架能将平均风险触发率从28.3%提升至52.6%，揭示了当前自主智能体系统存在的系统性架构级漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞分析主要关注单轮、无状态行为，忽略了具有状态的多轮交互和动态工具调用所带来的扩展攻击面，因此亟需系统性地量化LLM智能体系统面临的新型安全威胁。

Method: 提出了多维规避框架，包含三种攻击向量：时间规避（跨多轮交互分片恶意负载）、空间规避（将负载隐藏在复杂外部工件中绕过LLM解析）、语义规避（用良性上下文噪声掩盖恶意意图）。构建了包含2,254条真实智能体执行轨迹的A3S-Bench基准数据集，在10个主流LLM后端和20个实际威胁场景下进行评估。

Result: 该规避框架将平均风险触发率从28.3%的基线提升至52.6%，显著暴露了现有防御机制无法应对的系统性漏洞。

Conclusion: 当前自主智能体系统存在架构级安全漏洞，现有防御手段难以应对这些独特威胁，迫切需要设计专门针对智能体系统的防御机制。

Abstract: As autonomous agents (e.g., OpenClaw) increasingly operate with deep system-level privileges to execute complex tasks, they introduce severe, unmitigated security risks. Current vulnerability analyses overwhelmingly focus on single-turn, stateless behaviors, overlooking the expanded attack surface inherent in stateful, multi-turn interactions and dynamic tool invocations. In this paper, we propose a novel, multi-dimensional evasion framework targeting LLM-based agent systems. We introduce three stealthy attack vectors: (1) Temporal evasion, which fragments malicious payloads across sequential interaction turns; (2) Spatial evasion, which conceals payloads within complex external artifacts that evade standard LLM parsing mechanisms; and (3) Semantic evasion, which obscures malicious intents beneath benign contextual noise. To systematically quantify these threats, we construct A3S-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,254 real-world agent execution trajectories. Evaluating a standard agent framework separately integrated with 10 mainstream LLM backbones against 20 practical threat scenarios, we demonstrate that our evasion framework elevates the average risk trigger rate from a 28.3\% baseline to 52.6\%. These findings reveal systemic, architecture-level vulnerabilities in current autonomous agent systems that existing defenses fail to address, highlighting an urgent need for defense mechanisms tailored to the unique threats.

</details>


### [75] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种帕累托感知的触发式主动学习框架，用于降低安全运营中心的误报负担。它在冻结的XGBoost-Focal分类器上添加自适应窗口评分漂移触发和混合采集策略，在两个低流行率基准数据集上，相比冻结基线降低43%和21%的误报负担，同时比周期性随机更新减少3.8倍和5.2倍分析师查询量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低流行率数据流中，即使很低的误报率也会产生大量调查负担，而聚合F1分数无法体现分析师的实际工作负荷。

Method: PACT框架包含三个组件：(1) 冻结的XGBoost-Focal筛选器作为基础分类器；(2) 自适应窗口评分漂移触发机制；(3) 结合阈值相对不确定性与高分采样的混合采集规则。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个公共基准上，PACT达到自适应方法中最低的良态归一化误报负担。相比冻结基线降低43%和21%负担，相比周期性随机更新减少3.8倍和5.2倍查询量。消融实验显示采集策略贡献独立于触发时机，但自由运行触发下正窗口召回下降约10个百分点。

Conclusion: PACT有效平衡了误报负担与召回率，纯误报最小化策略虽能进一步降低负担，但会导致BOTSv1召回率崩溃式下降55个百分点，说明在现有工作负载假设下不能为降低负担而过度牺牲召回率。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [76] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对远程MCP服务器的认证安全性进行大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器未经验证即暴露工具，且所有经过OAuth认证的服务器均存在至少一种安全缺陷，共识别325个漏洞，已获得9个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP正成为LLM与外部服务连接的关键接口，远程部署中认证边界成为安全关键但尚未被充分研究。

Method: 识别7973个活跃远程MCP服务器，提出包含4类9种具体缺陷类型的认证缺陷分类体系，实现结合被动流量检测与主动动态探测的半自动化检测框架，对119个可测试的OAuth MCP服务器进行评估。

Result: 40.55%服务器无认证暴露工具；所有119个OAuth服务器均至少存在一个缺陷，共发现325个缺陷，其中动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%服务器；已获得9个CVE编号。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统存在普遍的认证安全弱点，亟需强化基于OAuth的远程部署安全措施。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [77] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列预测（TSF）中的后门攻击防御问题，系统评估了13种现有防御方法并揭示了两个根本缺陷：数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释，任务形式转换导致训练损失退化。基于此提出TimeGuard防御方法，采用通道级池训练范式，结合时间感知准则初始化高置信度池，并引入距离正则化损失选择机制，在多个数据集和攻击场景下将鲁棒性提升1.96倍，同时保持干净性能损失在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSF在各领域至关重要但易受后门攻击，然而针对TSF的后门防御研究严重不足，主要面临数据纠缠和任务形式转换两大挑战，现有防御方法直接迁移效果不佳。

Method: 提出TimeGuard防御方法：采用通道级池训练（channel-wise pool training）为核心范式；利用时间感知准则初始化高置信度池以缓解信号稀释；引入距离正则化损失选择机制，在训练过程中逐步扩展可靠池并缓解损失退化问题。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard的MAE_P（后门样本指标）相比最优基线提升1.96倍，同时干净样本性能MAE_C下降控制在5%以内。

Conclusion: 系统评估揭示了现有TSF后门防御失效的根本原因，提出的TimeGuard有效解决了数据纠缠和任务形式转换两大挑战，为TSF领域提供了首个针对性的训练时后门防御方案。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [78] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于少量已知良性客户端作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度-based低维梯度聚类、增强聚类低维梯度生成器和低维梯度重聚类三步骤，有效抵御动态模型投毒攻击，在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上展现了优越的保真度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临日益严重的模型投毒攻击威胁，现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数，难以适应恶意客户端的动态投毒策略，且常因客户端本地数据集的异质性导致良性梯度丢失。

Method: 1) 基于密度的低维梯度聚类：将梯度投影到两个最大发散维度，应用密度聚类识别恶意梯度，保留聚类内良性梯度和潜在的良性离群点；2) 增强聚类低维梯度生成器：学习生成与良性聚类边界对齐的伪梯度，作为连接稀疏良性梯度离群点的桥梁；3) 低维梯度重聚类：将生成的伪梯度与真实梯度一起聚类，恢复被误分类为噪声点的良性梯度。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下展现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法利用少量已知良性客户端作为参考，能够准确识别和过滤恶意梯度，同时尽可能保留良性梯度，即使在恶意客户端数量未知且可变的情况下也能有效工作。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [Measuring Security Without Fooling Ourselves: Why Benchmarking Agents Is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22568)
*Sahar Abdelnabi,Chris Hicks,Konrad Rieck,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出当前用于评估安全关键角色中AI智能体的基准测试存在三个核心缺陷：基准漏洞、时间滞后性和运行时不确定性，并提出了构建更鲁棒、可信评估框架的实用方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在评估安全关键型AI智能体时存在严重缺陷，可能导致对AI安全性能的误判，进而造成实际部署中的安全风险。

Method: 基于近期实证证据，系统性地识别和分类了安全评估中面临的三个核心挑战，并针对每个挑战提出了改进方向。

Result: 明确了三个核心挑战：（1）基准漏洞——基准测试本身可被利用或绕过；（2）时间滞后性——基准测试无法跟上AI能力的快速演进；（3）运行时不确定性——评估结果无法可靠预测AI在实际部署环境中的行为。

Conclusion: 需要构建动态更新、对抗鲁棒且能反映运行时不确定性的新型评估框架，以确保安全关键型AI智能体的可信评估。

Abstract: The benchmarks used to evaluate AI agents in security-critical roles suffer from crucial weaknesses. Building on recent empirical evidence, we characterize three core challenges that undermine security evaluations: benchmark vulnerabilities, temporal staleness, and runtime uncertainty. We then outline practical directions toward building more robust and trustworthy evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [80] [A Formal Basis for Quantum Cryptographic Exposure Measurement under HNDL Threat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22569)
*Matheus Rufino,Rafael Duarte Marcelino,Julio Smanioto Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明HNDL泄露概率的函数形式并非校准选择，而是由三个关于对抗生成和价值衰减动态的假设所结构性地决定，并据此构建了一个基于结构的操作化暴露优先级框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化"先复制后解密"威胁的暴露程度，揭示现有加性评分框架在结构上的根本缺陷。

Method: 通过三个假设推导HNDL泄露概率的因子分解形式，包含时间风险、密码学脆弱性与操作暴露的乘法项、以及攻防强度比决定的分母饱和项。

Result: HNDL概率因子分解为时间风险项 × 密码学脆弱性与操作暴露的乘积极限项 / 攻防强度比饱和项；各维度的边际敏感度内生依赖于组织在脆弱性-暴露平面中的位置。

Conclusion: 加性评分框架因无法捕捉密码学脆弱性与操作暴露之间的交互作用而结构性地失效；所提框架为部分可观测条件下的HNDL暴露优先级排序提供了结构化的理论基础。

Abstract: An adversary copies your encrypted traffic today and waits for a quantum computer to decrypt it later. How exposed are you?
  We show that the functional form of the answer is not merely a calibration choice -- it is structurally justified by three assumptions about adversarial production and value-decay dynamics. Under those assumptions, the HNDL compromise probability factorises into a temporal hazard, a multiplicative cryptographic-vulnerability and operational-exposure term, and a saturation denominator governed by the defense-attack intensity ratio; the marginal sensitivity to each dimension is endogenous to the organisation's position in the vulnerability-exposure plane, not a fixed global constant. Additive scoring frameworks cannot reproduce this structure because the interaction between cryptographic vulnerability and operational exposure is absent by construction, regardless of calibration. The resulting framework provides a structurally grounded basis for operational HNDL exposure prioritisation under partial observability.

</details>


### [81] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS，一个用于操作技术(OT)渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台，填补了OT领域缺乏类似Kali Linux这样集成化安全工具平台的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全专业人员有Kali Linux等现成开源平台，但支撑工业控制系统的操作技术(OT)领域缺乏类似的集成化开源安全测试平台。

Method: 作者架构、构建并发布了LINICS——一个用于OT渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台，并分享了相关经验。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS开源平台，为OT安全测试提供了集成化工具环境。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT领域缺乏开源安全测试平台的空白，为OT渗透测试和安全分析提供了类似于Kali Linux的集成化解决方案。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


### [82] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep提出了一种四无人机编队架构，用于在0.25-1.5米距离内对嵌入式微控制器进行自主电磁边信道分析。通过三架专用采集无人机（全频谱锚点、掩码寄存器加载探测、掩码SubBytes输出探测）和一架汇聚无人机（相干合并+4.8 dB信噪比增益、二阶掩码消除），在ANSSI ASCAD数据集上模拟实现了主掩码数据集0.25米处密钥秩18±1.7，并利用互相关对齐补偿了无人机悬停振动带来的去同步问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁边信道分析假设探头静止且贴近目标，低估了空中 adversaries 的威胁。本文旨在填补这一空白，设计并评估基于无人机编队的远距离自主EM-SCA方案。

Method: 提出TriSweep仿真框架，采用四无人机编队：Anchor（全频谱采集）、Mask Probe（掩码寄存器加载泄漏采集）、Cipher Probe（掩码SubBytes输出泄漏采集）三架专用采集无人机，以及一架Accumulator汇聚无人机进行相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和基于两个空间分离泄漏流的中心化乘积二阶掩码消除。使用互相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，并采用双通道CNN进行后处理。

Result: 在ANSSI ASCAD数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128及50/100样本去同步变体）上，主掩码数据集0.25米处实现密钥秩18±1.7。单无人机在100样本抖动变体上通过互相关对齐，密钥秩从89降至21。双通道CNN在去同步数据集上收敛至损失0.454（随机基线5.545）。

Conclusion: TriSweep仿真结果证明四无人机编队架构在远距离EM-SCA中的可行性，通过空间分集和相干合并有效提升信噪比并消除掩码。但尚未进行物理硬件原型构建，这是下一步计划。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MOOD的基准，用于系统评估LLM监控管线能否检测到分布外（OOD）的对齐失败。研究发现守卫模型难以泛化到OOD场景，而将守卫模型与OOD检测器（如马氏距离和困惑度检测器）结合可将召回率从39%提升至45%，且OOD检测带来的增益比单纯扩大20倍参数的守卫模型更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多LLM的安全和对齐失败源于分布外（OOD）情况，即模型开发者未预见到的异常提示或响应模式。现有研究缺乏系统评估监控管线能否检测这些OOD对齐失败的基准和方法。

Method: 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和七个分布外的多样化对齐失败测试集；测试守卫模型（安全分类器）及其与四种OOD检测器的组合方案（包括马氏距离、困惑度等）。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化能力差；将守卫模型与马氏距离和困惑度结合的OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测带来的召回率增益优于单纯将守卫模型参数扩大20倍的效果。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组成部分，为后续相关研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [84] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个多智能体AI框架，能够将自然语言设计意图转化为迭代拓扑优化流程，在60%的试验中生成符合审美偏好的结构设计，并支持从意图到原型制造的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化中，设计师需要手动将视觉风格、产品体验等定性设计意图转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器参数设置，缺乏直观的意图-参数映射机制。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，包含：1) 将自然语言问题描述转化为验证后的求解器输入；2) 运行拓扑优化求解器并渲染3D拓扑结果；3) 使用多视图视觉-语言推理与独立评审智能体对结果进行评判并迭代修正参数；4) 制造智能体对优选设计进行增材制造后处理。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准测试和手机支架产品设计两个任务中，针对层级分支结构的美学偏好，经过4轮迭代修正，TO-Agents在60%的试验中生成符合偏好的设计；相比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管道，成功试验次数提升最多6倍；评审得分和人工评估表明系统能识别有效参数杠杆、从不良修正中恢复并扩展设计探索空间。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的拓扑优化能够将设计师从低层级参数调优转向高层级形式与功能规范，同时需建立可靠自主工程设计所需的安全保障机制。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [85] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过对USC犹太大屠杀基金会和耶鲁大学Fortunoff视频档案馆的1600多份证词进行大规模计算分析，量化了两种口述证词的结构化程度，证实了二者存在结构性差异的同时也发现了显著重叠，挑战了简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二分法，并为数字口述历史与比较语料库分析提供了可扩展、可复现的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究中长期存在一种观点：USC证词倾向于结构化、采访者引导的格式，而耶鲁Fortunoff档案馆则更偏向自由开放的形式。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案馆的建设，但缺乏大规模量化验证。本研究旨在通过计算分析方法系统地检验这一基础性论断。

Method: 对来自两个档案集的1600多份证词进行大规模计算分析，采用话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型的分析方法，通过主题连贯性、采访者与幸存者之间的互动动态以及问题类型的分布来量化证词的"结构化"程度。

Result: 研究结果总体上证实了早期研究所识别的结构性差异，但同时也揭示了两组档案之间存在显著的重叠——无论是在单个访谈的内部还是跨共同的叙事模式上，从而复杂化了以往简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二分法。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复现的比较语料库分析框架。作为概念验证，该框架可广泛应用于数字口述历史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台的设计。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [86] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MindLoom框架，通过将推理问题分解为原子知识-推理转换（思维模式）来合成前沿水平的推理数据，在9个基准测试中取得优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对问题难度的结构因素可见性有限，导致数据多样性窄、难度控制不稳定，难以系统性地生成前沿水平的推理数据。

Method: MindLoom框架：1) 将困难问题的已验证解分解为思维模式链；2) 训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配；3) 迭代应用检索到的思维模式组合新问题，配合分布对齐采样；4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并提供经过评判的正确响应用于SFT。

Result: 在9个基准（5个STEM学科、4个数学推理任务）上，使用多种模型族和规模，MindLoom微调模型优于基础模型、蒸馏和外部数据基线。消融实验验证了各组件贡献，分析表明MindLoom覆盖广泛推理模式并保持有效的难度控制。

Conclusion: 通过思维模式组合工程，MindLoom能够系统性地合成多样且难度可控的前沿推理数据，有效提升LLM推理能力。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [87] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，这是第三个证据模型原型，旨在通过数据模型扩展、证据结构化提升FAIR性和AI就绪度，以及增强AOP框架与NAMs的集成，解决AOP-Wiki在支持计算生成AOP和定量AOP方面的数据模型和应用基础设施限制，以服务于监管科学、生物医学和One Health领域的AOP应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AOP-Wiki的数据模型和应用基础设施限制了AOP的持续增长和演化，无法充分支持计算生成AOP、定量AOP以及下一代风险评估中对NAMs集成的需求，亟需进行现代化改造。

Method: 利用主体AI的变革力量，结合核心AOP原则，设计并实现AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0原型，专注于：1) AOP-Wiki内部质量改进；2) 证据结构化以增强FAIR性和AI就绪度；3) 改进AOP框架与NAMs之间的集成。

Result: 提出了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0原型，具体展示了数据模型扩展方案，并阐述了AOP-Wiki的转型愿景，以更好地服务监管科学以及AOP在生物医学和One Health领域的新兴应用。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0为支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP奠定了基础，通过提升数据质量、FAIR性和AI就绪度，以及加强AOP与NAMs的集成，为下一代风险评估提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [88] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过向AI系统提问不合理类别成员（如“橄榄是车辆吗？”）来探究概念边界，并与人类判断进行比较，发现AI在概念理解上与人类存在显著差异，例如将“词语”归类为“车辆”或“服装”，将某些“蔬菜”归为“水果”，且这种概念错位会引发AI安全相关的下游问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有人类般概念理解的AI系统是构建安全可靠系统的关键步骤。现有方法通过询问合理类别成员来探测概念理解，容易受训练数据模式影响；本文采用询问不合理类别成员的替代策略，以更准确地刻画概念边界。

Method: 基于Rosch和Mervis经典心理学研究中的基本概念，让AI系统对物体进行上级类别归属判断，包括正常类别归属和错误类别归属任务，并将结果与人类参与者的判断进行比较。

Result: AI系统在多个概念上与人类存在显著差异：将“词语”归类为“车辆”和“服装”，将部分“蔬菜”识别为“水果”，将非武器类别的样本分配给“武器”类别。

Conclusion: AI系统与人类在概念边界理解上存在有意义且令人惊讶的差异，这些概念错位会转化为有问题的下游行为，对AI安全性构成影响。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [89] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过逻辑推理任务实验，发现AI使用强度与个体技能发展呈负相关：重度AI使用者表现弱于同龄人，而轻度使用者与不使用AI者表现相似。AI的信息含量是中介因素——低信息AI损害学习，高信息AI短期提升表现但长期效果存在异质性。AI既可互补也可替代人类技能发展，需合理调控AI使用以促进技能培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI融入人类问题解决过程后，对个体技能发展的影响机制尚不明确，特别是AI使用强度和信息含量如何调节学习效果。

Method: 在受控逻辑推理任务中，被试可按需获取AI辅助，通过比较不同AI使用强度（重度/轻度/不使用）和信息含量（高/低）对即时表现和后续无AI辅助表现的影响。

Result: 重度AI使用者的技能发展显著弱于同龄人；低信息AI既不改善即时表现，移除后表现更差；高信息AI短期提升表现，但长期效果具有异质性。

Conclusion: AI对技能发展的影响取决于上下文：高信息AI可补充独立推理能力（互补效应），低信息AI则替代削弱推理能力（替代效应）；需通过调控AI的可及性与使用方式来促进技能培养。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [90] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将拒绝抑制问题重新定义为隐空间逃逸攻击，即对线性探测器的逃逸。现有方法通过消减拒绝方向本质上是投影到决策边界，而本文提出受控隐空间逃逸攻击(CLE)，将表示投影越过边界并优化置信度，在15个模型上取得SOTA攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐模型的拒绝行为抑制方法（如消减拒绝方向）缺乏对隐空间变换的 principled 解释，不清楚为何能抑制拒绝。

Method: 1. 将拒绝抑制重新形式化为隐空间逃逸攻击；2. 证明先前工作的差值均值方向定义了一个线性探测器，消减操作等价于投影到决策边界；3. 提出受控隐空间逃逸攻击(CLE)，将表示投影越过决策边界并优化置信度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上取得SOTA攻击成功率，超越现有的拒绝消减基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 隐空间逃逸攻击视角不仅解释了先前工作的成功原理（投影到决策边界），还揭示了其局限性（停在边界），推动了更有效的受控逃逸攻击。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [91] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于200轮真实人机多轮对话的情感智能基准测试，发现情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断是相互独立的能力，其中偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确性更能区分模型优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接衡量模型在真实多轮对话中推断和响应用户情感状态的能力。

Method: 基于200轮真实多轮人机对话，让参与者与匿名LLM对话并提供逐轮的情感状态、模型行为和偏好响应标注，评估11个模型在情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断上的表现。

Result: 模型在各任务上的排名大致独立，说明情感智能行为可分解为独立能力。偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确性更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在上下文中期望的响应类型，聚合评分可能掩盖这一区别，而单轮或合成格式无法跨轮直接捕捉。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [92] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度实质上反映了平台用户基础而非劳动力市场特征。通过固定其他变量仅改变平台输入，ChatGPT后的就业系数变化达1.9倍，消费者与企业渠道的估计符号相反。按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后，估计值衰减42%-93%。非经典测量误差导致替代弹性被低估的程度大于互补弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用AI平台对话日志来测量职业暴露度，但该方法可能因平台用户构成与劳动力市场构成的差异而产生系统性偏差。本文旨在量化这一偏差并揭示其影响机制。

Method: 固定结果、样本、控制变量和估计量，仅改变平台输入来源；比较消费者与企业渠道的估计差异；使用BLS劳动力份额重新加权；形式化非经典测量误差模型并推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 仅改变平台输入使ChatGPT后就业系数变化1.9倍；消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反；BLS加权后估计衰减42%-93%；非经典测量误差导致替代弹性低估大于互补弹性。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台日志的职业暴露度测量存在严重的测量误差问题，该误差并非经典形式，会导致对技术对劳动力替代效应的系统性低估，需要谨慎使用并加以校正。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [93] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合因果发现与双极论证框架(BAF)的新方法，利用半稳定语义识别特征扩展，以解释模型为何做出特定预测，在基准数据集上优于标准后验可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法只能识别哪些特征与模型预测相关，但无法解释为何模型做出特定决策，缺乏对预测背后因果推理过程的阐明。

Method: 首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，然后将这些关系转化为双极论证框架(BAF)以表示特征间的支持与反对交互，最后采用半稳定语义(semi-stable semantics)寻找能够解释预测结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了该方法，并与标准后验可解释性方法进行了对比，取得了更好的解释效果。

Conclusion: 因果推理与论证机制的融合能够提供比传统相关性解释更深入、更具说服力的模型决策解释。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [94] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI谄媚行为缺乏统一定义，通过综述70篇论文建立分类体系（区分对用户信念/个人特质的谄媚，以及显性/隐性表达方式），并通过对106位专家的调查发现专家虽普遍认同谄媚是重要问题，但对具体行为是否属于谄媚存在严重分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚作为一个术语缺乏一致定义，研究者用同一术语描述不同行为导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对不同形式谄媚的抵抗力参差不齐。

Method: 1) 综述70篇AI谄媚相关论文，建立行为定义与测量的分类体系；2) 对106位AI谄媚及相关领域专家进行问卷调查，考察研究者对哪些模型行为属于谄媚的一致程度。

Result: 分类体系区分了谄媚的两个维度：(1) 对用户立场/信念 vs. 对用户个人特质/情感；(2) 通过显性直接语言 vs. 隐性间接行为（如框架、省略、语气）。现有研究偏向于对用户信念的显性谄媚，隐性及针对个人的行为研究不足。94.3%专家认为谄媚是当前AI系统的重要问题，但对具体行为是否属于谄媚存在严重分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一类广泛的行为家族，不同行为具有不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理影响。所提出的分类体系为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [95] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL框架，从人群偏好中提取安全对齐的技能，用于下游强化学习任务，在无显式安全奖励的情况下降低安全成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中用户偏好数据隐含安全标准，但直接组合偏好学习奖励与任务奖励存在局限性，需要更有效的方法从人群偏好中发现共享安全准则并迁移到下游任务。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL层次化框架，先从人群偏好中提取安全对齐的技能（safety-aligned skills），再通过高层策略组合这些技能以安全地解决下游任务。

Result: 在安全RL环境和LLM风格任务上，该方法在没有显式安全奖励的情况下显著降低了安全成本，任务性能与使用真实安全信号的oracle方法相当。

Conclusion: 层次化技能提取与组合的方式比直接奖励组合更有效地利用人群偏好中的共享安全准则，实现安全与任务性能的平衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [96] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端智能体框架（VIS co-scientist），仅需数据和高级任务描述，即可自主设计并生成定制化的可视化分析应用（VIS Apps），在IEEE SciVis竞赛数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中检查、解释和沟通复杂数据的能力至关重要，但往往需要超出核心领域的专业知识（数据管理、分析、可视化设计与实现）。现有方法缺乏能根据高层指令自主完成长周期任务的系统，因此需要一种更通用的AI协同科学家。

Method: 提出VIS co-scientist框架，包含多个智能体和专业技能的协调：探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证，以及最重要的整体任务完成评估。各阶段生成文档和指令产物，指导下游工作并支持迭代优化。

Result: 在多个科学和工程领域的IEEE SciVis竞赛上验证，系统仅凭数据和目标任务即可自主生成功能完整的单页面可视化应用，具有经过验证的联动视图行为，且高度定制化以满足领域专家的特定任务和需求。

Conclusion: 该工作是迈向通用AI协同科学家愿景的重要一步，展示了自主数据分析和可视化解决方案设计的能力，为处理真实世界复杂任务（模糊需求、多样数据模态、设计权衡、任务驱动验证）提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields.
